    FROM [ReadingProgress] AS [r]
    WHERE ([r].[IdUserDelete] IS NULL OR [r].[IdUserDelete] LIKE N'') AND [r].[DeleteDate] IS NULL AND [r].[UserId] = @__request_UserId_0
    GROUP BY [r].[MangaId]
    ORDER BY MAX([r].[LastReadAt]) DESC
    OFFSET @__p_1 ROWS FETCH NEXT @__p_2 ROWS ONLY
) AS [t]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [t1].[MangaId], [t1].[MangaName], [t1].[MangaThumbnail], [t1].[ChapterId], [t1].[ChapterTitle], [t1].[ChapterIndex], [t1].[LastPage], [t1].[LastReadAt], [t1].[c]

        SELECT [r0].[MangaId], [t0].[Name] AS [MangaName], [t0].[MangaThumbnail], [r0].[ChapterId], [t3].[Title] AS [ChapterTitle], [t3].[Index] AS [ChapterIndex], [r0].[LastPage], [r0].[LastReadAt], 1 AS [c], ROW_NUMBER() OVER(PARTITION BY [r0].[MangaId] ORDER BY [r0].[LastReadAt] DESC) AS [row]
        FROM [ReadingProgress] AS [r0]
        INNER JOIN (
            SELECT [m].[Id], [m].[MangaThumbnail], [m].[Name]
            FROM [Manga] AS [m]

        INNER JOIN (

    ) AS [t1]
    WHERE [t1].[row] <= 1

    WHERE ([r].[IdUserDelete] IS NULL OR [r].[IdUserDelete] LIKE N'') AND [r].[DeleteDate] IS NULL AND [r].[UserId] = @__request_UserId_0

    ORDER BY MAX([r].[LastReadAt]) DESC
    OFFSET @__p_1 ROWS FETCH NEXT @__p_2 ROWS ONLY
) AS [t]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [t1].[MangaId], [t1].[MangaName], [t1].[MangaThumbnail], [t1].[ChapterId], [t1].[ChapterTitle], [t1].[ChapterIndex], [t1].[LastPage], [t1].[LastReadAt], [t1].[c]

        SELECT [r0].[MangaId], [t0].[Name] AS [MangaName], [t0].[MangaThumbnail], [r0].[ChapterId], [t3].[Title] AS [ChapterTitle], [t3].[Index] AS [ChapterIndex], [r0].[LastPage], [r0].[LastReadAt], 1 AS [c], ROW_NUMBER() OVER(PARTITION BY [r0].[MangaId] ORDER BY [r0].[LastReadAt] DESC) AS [row]

        ) AS [t3] ON [r0].[ChapterId] = [t3].[Id]

    ) AS [t1]
    WHERE [t1].[row] <= 1
) AS [t2] ON [t].[MangaId] = [t2].[MangaId]
